### Folio 28 (`Page ID: 5509-28` | `158` Words | `4` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> إِضَافَةُ الْحَوْقَلَةِ إِلَى الْبَاقِيَاتِ الصَّالِحَاتِ وَقَدْ تَقَدَّمَ فِي حَدِيثِ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيِّ ﵁ إِضَافَةُ لَا حَوْلَ وَلا قُوَّةَ إِلا بِاللَّهِ إِلَيْهَاوَكَذَلِكَ ذَكَرَهَا عُثْمَانُ ﵁ فِي تَفْسِيرِهِ لَهَا أَعْنِي الْبَاقِيَاتِ الصَّالِحَاتِ كَمَا تَقَدَّمَوَمَعْنَاهَا اخْتِصَاصُ الرَّبِّ ﷾ بِالْمَشِيئَةِ وَالْقُدْرَةِ وَسَلْبُ الْعَبْدِ عَنْ كُلِّ اخْتِيَارٍ وَتصرف إِلَّا بمشيئته سُبْحَانَهُ تَعَالَى فِي مَشِيئَتِهِ عَلَى نِهَايَةِ التَّفْوِيضِ وَخَالَصِهِوَلِهَذَا جَعَلَهَا النَّبِيُّ ﷺ مِنْ كُنُوزِ الْجَنَّةِ فِي الْحَدِيثِ الَّذِي أَخْبَرَنَاهُ عَلِيُّ بْنُ يَحْيَى بْنِ عَلِيٍّ الْمُعَدَّلُ أَنا أَحْمَدُ بْنُ الْمُفَرّجِ الأُمَوِيُّ سَمَاعًا(ح) وَأَخْبَرَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ مميلٍ الْمِزِّيُّ بِهَا عَنْ أَبِي مُحَمَّدٍ الْحَسَنِ بْنِ عَلِيِّ بْنِ الْمُرْتَضَى الْعَلَوِيِّ الْعَاصِمِيِّ أَنا عَبْدُ الْوَاحِدِ بْنُ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ مَهْدِيٍّ ثَنَا الْحُسَيْنُ بْنُ إِسْمَاعِيلَ الْمَحَامِلِيُّ ثَنَا يَعْقُوبُ بْنُ إِبْرَاهِيمَ الدَّوْرَقِيُّ ثَنَا أَبُو مُعَاوِيَةَ الضَّرِيرُ ثَنَا عَاصِمٌ الأَحْوَلُ عَنْ أَبِي عُثْمَانَ عَنْ أَبِي مُوسَى ﵁ قَالَكُنَّا مَعَ النَّبِيِّ ﷺ فِي سَفَرٍ فَقَالَ يَا عَبْدَ اللَّهِ بْنَ قَيْسٍ أَلا أَدُلُّكَ عَلَى كَلِمَةٍ مِنْ كَنْزِ الْجَنَّةِقَالَ قُلْتُ بَلَى قَالَ لَا حَوْلَ وَلا قُوَّةَ إِلا بِاللَّهِ

**English**:
> Construct the Ḥūqlah to the remain s righteous deeds certainly, already His statement in modern Abī Saʿīd the Khadrī (may Allah be pleased with him) construct not power (ḥawl) and not strength (quwwah) except in Allah to it that mentioned (Dhikr) her ʿUthmān (may Allah be pleased with him) in his Qur'ānic Exegesis of to I mean the remain s righteous deeds just as His statement and with, together with her specific the Lord of ((Mighty and Majestic is He)) in the will (mashīʾah) and the power Wsalb the servant (al-ʿAbd) from every good and turn away, avert except in his will (mashīʾah) Glorified is He (Exalted is He) in His Will upon forbid the Tafwīḍ his Wkhālṣ and for this made her the Prophet (may Allah bless him and grant him peace) from the treasures of Paradise (al-Jannah) in Modern the one who informed us it ʿAlī ibn Yaḥyā ibn ʿAlī the justice I Aḥmad ibn al-Mufrij al-Umawī by attested usage (samāʿan) (Ḥ) and report us Muḥammad ibn Muḥammad ibn Mumīil al-Mazī by from Abī Muḥammad al-Ḥasan ibn ʿAlī ibn al-Murtiḍī al-ʿalūī the ʿāṣimī I ʿAbd al-Wāḥid ibn Muḥammad ibn Mahdī narrated to us [thanā] al-Ḥusayn ibn Ismāʿīl al-Maḥāmlī narrated to us [thanā] Yaʿqūb ibn Ibrāhīm al-Dūrqī narrated to us [thanā] Abū Muʿʾiwiyyah the harm narrated to us [thanā] ʿĀṣim ibn Abī al-Najūd al-Kūfī (Canonical Reciter) the state from Abī ʿUthmān from Abī Mūsā (may Allah be pleased with him) said be with the Prophet (may Allah bless him and grant him peace) in journey so he said O ʿAbdullāh ibn Qays Unquestionably... not your Adl upon Word from is a treasure Paradise (al-Jannah) said I said Yes, indeed said not power (ḥawl) and not strength (quwwah) except in Allah

**Sentence-by-Sentence Bilingual Alignment**:
- **[S1] AR**: إِضَافَةُ الْحَوْقَلَةِ إِلَى الْبَاقِيَاتِ الصَّالِحَاتِ وَقَدْ تَقَدَّمَ فِي حَدِيثِ أَبِي سَعِيدٍ الْخُدْرِيِّ ﵁ إِضَافَةُ لَا حَوْلَ وَلا قُوَّةَ إِلا بِاللَّهِ إِلَيْهَا وَكَذَلِكَ ذَكَرَهَا عُثْمَانُ ﵁ فِي تَفْسِيرِهِ لَهَا أَعْنِي الْبَاقِيَاتِ الصَّالِحَاتِ كَمَا تَقَدَّمَ وَمَعْنَاهَا اخْتِصَاصُ الرَّبِّ ﷾ بِالْمَشِيئَةِ وَالْقُدْرَةِ وَسَلْبُ الْعَبْدِ عَنْ كُلِّ اخْتِيَارٍ وَتصرف
  **[S1] EN**: Construct the Ḥūqlah to the remain s righteous deeds certainly, already His statement in modern Abī Saʿīd the Khadrī (may Allah be pleased with him) construct not power (ḥawl) and not strength (quwwah) except in Allah to it that mentioned (Dhikr) her ʿUthmān (may Allah be pleased with him) in his Qur'ānic Exegesis of to I mean the remain s righteous deeds just as His statement and with, together with her specific the Lord of ((Mighty and Majestic is He)) in the will (mashīʾah) and the power Wsalb the servant (al-ʿAbd) from every good and turn away, avert

  **[S2] EN**: Except in his will (mashīʾah) Glorified is He (Exalted is He) in His Will upon forbid the Tafwīḍ his Wkhālṣ and for this made her the Prophet (may Allah bless him and grant him peace) from the treasures of Paradise (al-Jannah) in Modern the one who informed us it ʿAlī ibn Yaḥyā ibn ʿAlī the justice I Aḥmad ibn al-Mufrij al-Umawī by attested usage (samāʿan) (Ḥ) and report us Muḥammad ibn Muḥammad ibn Mumīil al-Mazī by from Abī Muḥammad

  **[S3] EN**: Al-Ḥasan ibn ʿAlī ibn al-Murtiḍī al-ʿalūī the ʿāṣimī I ʿAbd al-Wāḥid ibn Muḥammad ibn Mahdī narrated to us [thanā] al-Ḥusayn ibn Ismāʿīl al-Maḥāmlī narrated to us [thanā] Yaʿqūb ibn Ibrāhīm al-Dūrqī narrated to us [thanā] Abū Muʿʾiwiyyah the harm narrated to us [thanā] ʿĀṣim ibn Abī al-Najūd al-Kūfī (Canonical Reciter) the state from Abī ʿUthmān from Abī Mūsā (may Allah be pleased with him) said be with the Prophet (may Allah bless him and grant him peace) in journey

### Folio 29 (`Page ID: 5509-29` | `81` Words | `2` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> اتَّفَقَ الأَئِمَّةُ السِّتَّةُ عَلَيْهِ مِنْ عِدَّةِ طُرُقٍ أَطْوَلَ مِنْ هَذَا وَفِيهِ قِصَّةٌوَمِنْهَا رِوَايَةُ مُسْلِمٍ عَنْ أَبِي بَكْرِ بْنِ أَبِي شَيْبَةَ وَالنَّسَائِيِّ عَنْ أَحْمَدَ بْنِ حَرْبٍ كِلاهُمَا عَنْ أَبِي مُعَاوِيَةَ الضَّرِيرِ بِهِ فَوَقَعَ لَنَا بَدَلا لَهُمَا عَالِيًاوَبِهِ إِلَى الْمَحَامِلِيِّ قَالَ ثَنَا الْعَبَّاسُ بْنُ عَبْدِ اللَّهِ ثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ يُوسُفَ عَنْ سُفْيَانَ عَنِ الأَعْمَشِ عَنْ مُجَاهِدٍ عَن عبد الرَّحْمَن ابْن أَبِي لَيْلَى عَنْ أَبِي ذَرٍّ ﵁ قَالَقَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ لَا حَوْلَ وَلا قُوَّةَ إِلا بِاللَّهِ كَنْزٌ مِنْ كُنُوزِ الْجَنَّةِ

**English**:
> Agrees the leading Imāms / authorities (al-aʾimmah) the six upon him from the number (ʿiddah) of chains of transmission (ṭuruq) prolonged from this And in it narrate and among them is in the recension (riwāyah) of Muslim from Abī Bakr ibn Abī Shaybah and al-Nasāʾī from Aḥmad ibn Ḥarb their [dual] every from Abī Muʿʾiwiyyah the harm by happen, come to pass for us instead of place of (badalan) to haughty and by it (wa-bihi) to al-Maḥāmlī who said: Narrated to us [thanā] al-ʿAbbās ibn ʿAbdullāh narrated to us [thanā] Muḥammad ibn Yūsuf from Sufyān from the Aʿmash from Imām Mujāhid ibn Jabr (d. 104 AH) from ʿAbd al-Raḥmān Ibn Abī Līlī from Abī Dhar (may Allah be pleased with him) said said the Messenger of Allah (may Allah bless him and grant him peace) not power (ḥawl) and not strength (quwwah) except in Allah is a treasure from the treasures of Paradise (al-Jannah)

  **[S1] EN**: Agrees the leading Imāms / authorities (al-aʾimmah) the six upon him from the number (ʿiddah) of chains of transmission (ṭuruq) prolonged from this And in it narrate and among them is in the recension (riwāyah) of Muslim from Abī Bakr ibn Abī Shaybah and al-Nasāʾī from Aḥmad ibn Ḥarb their [dual] every from Abī Muʿʾiwiyyah the harm by happen, come to pass for us instead of place of (badalan) to haughty and by it (wa-bihi) to al-Maḥāmlī who said: Narrated to us [thanā] al-ʿAbbās ibn ʿAbdullāh
- **[S2] AR**: اللَّهِ ثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ يُوسُفَ عَنْ سُفْيَانَ عَنِ الأَعْمَشِ عَنْ مُجَاهِدٍ عَن عبد الرَّحْمَن ابْن أَبِي لَيْلَى عَنْ أَبِي ذَرٍّ ﵁ قَالَ قَالَ رَسُولُ اللَّهِ ﷺ لَا حَوْلَ وَلا قُوَّةَ إِلا بِاللَّهِ كَنْزٌ مِنْ كُنُوزِ الْجَنَّةِ
  **[S2] EN**: Narrated to us [thanā] Muḥammad ibn Yūsuf from Sufyān from the Aʿmash from Imām Mujāhid ibn Jabr (d. 104 AH) from ʿAbd al-Raḥmān Ibn Abī Līlī from Abī Dhar (may Allah be pleased with him) said said the Messenger of Allah (may Allah bless him and grant him peace) not power (ḥawl) and not strength (quwwah) except in Allah is a treasure from the treasures of Paradise (al-Jannah)

### Folio 30 (`Page ID: 5509-30` | `145` Words | `4` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> رَوَاهُ النَّسَائِيُّ وَابْنُ مَاجَهْ مِنْ حَدِيثِ يَحْيَى الْقَطَّانِ وَوَكِيعِ بْنِ الْجَرَّاحِ كِلاهُمَا عَنِ الثَّوْرِيِّ وَإِسْنَادُهُ صَحِيحٌوَقَدْ رَوَاهُ عَنْ أَبِي ذَرٍّ أَيْضًا عَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنُ غَنْمٍ وَبَشِيرُ بْنُ كَعْبٍ وَأَبُو زَيْنَبَ مَوْلَى حَازِمٍ الْغِفَارِيِّ مِنْ طُرُقٍوَرُوِيَ أَيْضًا مِنْ حَدِيثِ سَعْدِ بْنِ أَبِي وَقَّاصٍ وَمُعَاذِ بْنِ جَبَلٍ وَزَيْدِ بْنِ ثَابِتٍ وَأَبِي أَيُّوبَ الأَنْصَارِيِّ ﵃ وَغَيْرِهِمْ عَنِ النَّبِيِّ ﷺ مِنْ طُرُقٍ تَرَكْتُ ذِكْرَهَا خَوْفَ الإِطَالَةِ عِظَمُ مَوْقِعِ الْبَاقِيَاتِ الصَّالِحَاتِ وَلِعِظَمِ مَوْقَعِ هَذِهِ الْبَاقِيَاتِ الصَّالِحَاتِ جَعَلَهَا النَّبِيُّ ﷺ عَنِ الْقُرْآنِ الْعَزِيزِ فِي حَقِّ مَنْ لَا يُحْسِنُهُكَمَا أَخْبَرَنَا أَبُو الْفِدَاءِ إِسْمَاعِيلُ بْنُ يُوسُفَ الْمُقْرِئُ وَأَبُو مُحَمَّدٍ عِيسَى بْنُ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنِ مَعَالِي الْمُطْعِمُ قَالا أَنا عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ عُمَرَ الْحَرِيمِيُّ أَنا عَبْدُ الأَوَّلِ بْنُ عِيسَى الصُّوفِيُّ أَنا عَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنُ مُحَمَّدِ بْنِ الْمُظَفَّرِ أَنا عَبْدُ اللَّهِ بْنُ أَحْمَدَ بْنِ حَمُّوَيْهِ السَّرَخْسِيُّ أَنا إِبْرَاهِيمُ بْنُ خُزَيْمٍ ثَنَا عَبْدُ بْنُ حُمَيْدٍ ثَنَا أَبُو نُعَيْمٍ أَنا سُفْيَانُ عَنْ أَبِي خَالِدٍ

**English**:
> Narrated it al-Nasāʾī and Ibn Mājih from modern Yaḥyā the ever and Imām Wakīʿ ibn al-Jarāḥ their [dual] every from al-Thawrī and chain of transmission it sound certainly, already narrated it from Abī Dhar also (ayḍan) ʿAbd al-Raḥmān ibn Ghanm bringer ibn Kaʿb and Abū Zīnb protector, patron Ḥāzim the Ghafārī from chains of transmission (ṭuruq) and it was narrated (wa-ruwiya) also (ayḍan) from modern Saʿd ibn Abī Waqqāṣ and Muʿādh ibn Jabl Wazīd ibn Thābit and Abī Aīūb al-Anṣārī (may Allah be pleased with them both) their other than, not from the Prophet (may Allah bless him and grant him peace) from chains of transmission (ṭuruq) leave, abandon mentioned (Dhikr) her fear the prolonged greatness occur the remain s righteous deeds Wlaʿẓam occur this the remain s righteous deeds made her the Prophet (may Allah bless him and grant him peace) from the Qur'an the Mighty (al-ʿAzīz) in is true reality from not his Ḥasan just as informed us Abū al-Fadāʾ Ismāʿīl ibn Yūsuf the Master of Qur'ānic Recitation (al-Muqriʾ) and Abū Muḥammad ʿĪsā ibn ʿAbd al-Raḥmān ibn Muʿʾilī the food say I ʿAbdullāh ibn ʿUmar the worthy I ʿAbd al-Ūl ibn ʿĪsā the Ṣūfī I ʿAbd al-Raḥmān ibn Muḥammad ibn al-Muẓfir I ʿAbdullāh ibn Aḥmad ibn Ḥamūīh al-Sarakhsī I Ibrāhīm ibn Khazīm narrated to us [thanā] ʿAbd ibn Ḥamīd narrated to us [thanā] Abū Naʿīm I Sufyān from Abī Khālid

**Sentence-by-Sentence Bilingual Alignment**:
- **[S1] AR**: رَوَاهُ النَّسَائِيُّ وَابْنُ مَاجَهْ مِنْ حَدِيثِ يَحْيَى الْقَطَّانِ وَوَكِيعِ بْنِ الْجَرَّاحِ كِلاهُمَا عَنِ الثَّوْرِيِّ وَإِسْنَادُهُ صَحِيحٌ وَقَدْ رَوَاهُ عَنْ أَبِي ذَرٍّ أَيْضًا عَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنُ غَنْمٍ وَبَشِيرُ بْنُ كَعْبٍ وَأَبُو زَيْنَبَ مَوْلَى حَازِمٍ الْغِفَارِيِّ مِنْ طُرُقٍ وَرُوِيَ أَيْضًا مِنْ حَدِيثِ سَعْدِ بْنِ أَبِي وَقَّاصٍ وَمُعَاذِ
  **[S1] EN**: Narrated it al-Nasāʾī and Ibn Mājih from modern Yaḥyā the ever and Imām Wakīʿ ibn al-Jarāḥ their [dual] every from al-Thawrī and chain of transmission it sound certainly, already narrated it from Abī Dhar also (ayḍan) ʿAbd al-Raḥmān ibn Ghanm bringer ibn Kaʿb and Abū Zīnb protector, patron Ḥāzim the Ghafārī from chains of transmission (ṭuruq) and it was narrated (wa-ruwiya) also (ayḍan) from modern Saʿd ibn Abī Waqqāṣ and Muʿādh

  **[S2] EN**: Ibn Jabl Wazīd ibn Thābit and Abī Aīūb al-Anṣārī (may Allah be pleased with them both) their other than, not from the Prophet (may Allah bless him and grant him peace) from chains of transmission (ṭuruq) leave, abandon mentioned (Dhikr) her fear the prolonged greatness occur the remain s righteous deeds Wlaʿẓam occur this the remain s righteous deeds made her the Prophet (may Allah bless him and grant him peace) from the Qur'an the Mighty (al-ʿAzīz) in is true reality from not his Ḥasan just as informed us Abū al-Fadāʾ Ismāʿīl ibn

### Folio 31 (`Page ID: 5509-31` | `129` Words | `3` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> الْوَاسِطِيِّ عَنْ إِبْرَاهِيمَ وَلَيْسَ بِالنَّخَعِيِّ عَنِ ابْنِ أَبِي أَوْفَى ﵄ قَالَ:جَاءَ رَجُلٌ إِلَى النَّبِيِّ ﷺ فَقَالَ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ إِنِّي لَا أَسْتَطِيعُ أَنْ أَتَعَلَّمَ الْقُرْآنَ فَعَلِّمْنِي شَيْئًا يُجْزِينِي قَالَ تَقُولُسُبْحَانَ اللَّهِ وَالْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ وَلا إِلَهَ إِلا اللَّهُ وَاللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ وَلا حَوْلَ وَلا قُوَّةَ إِلا بِاللَّهِ فَقَالَ الأَعْرَابِيُّ هَكَذَا وَقَبَضَ يَدَيْهِ فَقَالَ هَذَا لِلَّهِ فَمَا لِيقَالَ تَقُولُ اللَّهُمَّ اغْفِرْ لِي وَارْحَمْنِي وَعَافِنِي وَارْزُقْنِي وَاهْدِنِيفَأَخَذَهَا الأَعْرَابِيُّ وَقَبَضَ كَفَّيْهِفَقَالَ النَّبِيُّ ﷺ أَمَّا هَذَا فَقَدْ مَلأَ يَدَيْهِ بِالْخَيْرِأَخْرَجَهُ أَبُو دَاوُدَ عَنْ عُثْمَانَ بْنِ أَبِي شَيْبَةَ عَنْ وَكِيعٍ عَنِ الثَّوْرِيِّ بِهِ وَرَوَاهُ النَّسَائِيُّ مِنْ حَدِيثِ مِسْعَرٍ عَن إِبْرَاهِيم السكْسكِي عَن أَبِي أَوْفَى بِهِ وَقَالَ إِبْرَاهِيمُ السَّكْسَكِيُّ لَيْسَ بِالْقَوِيِّقُلْتُ وَهُوَ رَاوِيهِ فِي هَذَا السَّنَدِ أَيْضًا وَهُوَ إِبْرَاهِيمُ بْنُ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ السَّكْسَكِيُّ احْتَجَّ بِهِ الْبُخَارِيُّ وَالْحَدِيثُ صَحَّحَهُ الدَّارَقُطْنِيُّ وَغَيْرُهُ

**English**:
> Al-Wāsiṭī from Ibrāhīm is not in al-Nakhaʿī from Ibn Abī Ūfī (may Allah be pleased with them) said: came a man (rajul) to the Prophet (may Allah bless him and grant him peace) so he said O the Messenger of Allah Indeed, I not Astaṭīʿ that Atʿalm the Qur'an know me anything recompense, reward me said say Glory be to Allah praise to Allah and not deity (ilāh) except Allah By Allah greater (akbar) and not power (ḥawl) and not strength (quwwah) except in Allah so he said al-Aʿrābī Thus and grants his two hands (yadayhi) so he said this to Allah so what for me me (lī) said say O Allah forgive for me me (lī) have mercy me me Waʿāf provide me and me Ahd so take, seize her al-Aʿrābī and grants his two palms (kaffayhi) so he said the Prophet (may Allah bless him and grant him peace) As for this certainly, already chiefs, leading assembly his two hands (yadayhi) good extracted it in his Hadith compilation (akhrajahu) Abū Dāwūd from ʿUthmān ibn Abī Shaybah from Wakīʿ from al-Thawrī by and narrated it al-Nasāʾī from modern blazing fire from Ibrāhīm the Saksakī from Abī Ūfī by and he said Ibrāhīm the Saksakī is not in al-Qūī I said and he narrator (rāwī) it in this the chain of transmission (al-Sanad) also (ayḍan) and he Ibrāhīm ibn ʿAbd al-Raḥmān the Saksakī Aḥtaj by al-Bukhārī and the modern authentic it al-Dāraquṭnī and others

**Sentence-by-Sentence Bilingual Alignment**:
- **[S1] AR**: الْوَاسِطِيِّ عَنْ إِبْرَاهِيمَ وَلَيْسَ بِالنَّخَعِيِّ عَنِ ابْنِ أَبِي أَوْفَى ﵄ قَالَ: جَاءَ رَجُلٌ إِلَى النَّبِيِّ ﷺ فَقَالَ يَا رَسُولَ اللَّهِ إِنِّي لَا أَسْتَطِيعُ أَنْ أَتَعَلَّمَ الْقُرْآنَ فَعَلِّمْنِي شَيْئًا يُجْزِينِي قَالَ تَقُولُ سُبْحَانَ اللَّهِ وَالْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ وَلا إِلَهَ إِلا اللَّهُ وَاللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ وَلا حَوْلَ وَلا قُوَّةَ
  **[S1] EN**: Al-Wāsiṭī from Ibrāhīm is not in al-Nakhaʿī from Ibn Abī Ūfī (may Allah be pleased with them) said: came a man (rajul) to the Prophet (may Allah bless him and grant him peace) so he said O the Messenger of Allah Indeed, I not Astaṭīʿ that Atʿalm the Qur'an know me anything recompense, reward me said say Glory be to Allah praise to Allah and not deity (ilāh) except Allah By Allah greater (akbar) and not power (ḥawl) and not strength (quwwah)
- **[S2] AR**: إِلا بِاللَّهِ فَقَالَ الأَعْرَابِيُّ هَكَذَا وَقَبَضَ يَدَيْهِ فَقَالَ هَذَا لِلَّهِ فَمَا لِي قَالَ تَقُولُ اللَّهُمَّ اغْفِرْ لِي وَارْحَمْنِي وَعَافِنِي وَارْزُقْنِي وَاهْدِنِي فَأَخَذَهَا الأَعْرَابِيُّ وَقَبَضَ كَفَّيْهِ فَقَالَ النَّبِيُّ ﷺ أَمَّا هَذَا فَقَدْ مَلأَ يَدَيْهِ بِالْخَيْرِ أَخْرَجَهُ أَبُو دَاوُدَ عَنْ عُثْمَانَ بْنِ أَبِي شَيْبَةَ عَنْ وَكِيعٍ عَنِ
  **[S2] EN**: Except in Allah so he said al-Aʿrābī Thus and grants his two hands (yadayhi) so he said this to Allah so what for me me (lī) said say O Allah forgive for me me (lī) have mercy me me Waʿāf provide me and me Ahd so take, seize her al-Aʿrābī and grants his two palms (kaffayhi) so he said the Prophet (may Allah bless him and grant him peace) As for this certainly, already chiefs, leading assembly his two hands (yadayhi) good extracted it in his Hadith compilation (akhrajahu) Abū Dāwūd from ʿUthmān ibn Abī Shaybah from Wakīʿ from
- **[S3] AR**: الثَّوْرِيِّ بِهِ وَرَوَاهُ النَّسَائِيُّ مِنْ حَدِيثِ مِسْعَرٍ عَن إِبْرَاهِيم السكْسكِي عَن أَبِي أَوْفَى بِهِ وَقَالَ إِبْرَاهِيمُ السَّكْسَكِيُّ لَيْسَ بِالْقَوِيِّ قُلْتُ وَهُوَ رَاوِيهِ فِي هَذَا السَّنَدِ أَيْضًا وَهُوَ إِبْرَاهِيمُ بْنُ عَبْدِ الرَّحْمَنِ السَّكْسَكِيُّ احْتَجَّ بِهِ الْبُخَارِيُّ وَالْحَدِيثُ صَحَّحَهُ الدَّارَقُطْنِيُّ وَغَيْرُهُ
  **[S3] EN**: Al-Thawrī by and narrated it al-Nasāʾī from modern blazing fire from Ibrāhīm the Saksakī from Abī Ūfī by and he said Ibrāhīm the Saksakī is not in al-Qūī I said and he narrator (rāwī) it in this the chain of transmission (al-Sanad) also (ayḍan) and he Ibrāhīm ibn ʿAbd al-Raḥmān the Saksakī Aḥtaj by al-Bukhārī and the modern authentic it al-Dāraquṭnī and others

### Folio 32 (`Page ID: 5509-32` | `138` Words | `4` Sentences)

**Arabic**:
> وَقَدْ رُوِيَ مِنْ حَدِيثِ سَعْدِ بْنِ أَبِي وَقَّاصٍ ﵁ أَيْضًاأَخْبَرَنَاهُ مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ أَحْمَدَ الزَّرَّادُ وَغَيْرُهُ أَنا يُوسُفُ ابْنُ بِنْتِ الْجَوْزِيِّ قَالَ أَنا جَدِّي الإِمَامُ أَبُو الْفَرَجِ عَبْدُ الرَّحْمَنِ بْنُ الْجَوْزِيِّ أَنا عَلِيُّ بْنُ عَبْدِ الْوَاحِدِ أَنا الْحَسَنُ بْنُ عَلِيٍّ أَنا عَلِيُّ بْنُ كَيْسَانَ النَّحْوِيُّ ثَنَا يُوسُفُ بْنُ يَعْقُوبَ الْقَاضِي ثَنَا مُحَمَّدُ بْنُ أَبِي بَكْرٍ ثَنَا عُمَرُ بْنُ عَلِيٍّ ثَنَا مُوسَى الْجُهَنِيّ عَن مُصعب بن سعد ابْن أَبِي وَقَّاصٍ عَنْ أَبِيهِ ﵁ قَالَ: جَاءَ أَعْرَابِيٌّ إِلَى النَّبِيِّ ﷺ فَقَالَ عَلِّمْنِي كَلامًا أَقُولُهُقَالَ قُلْ لَا إِلَهَ إِلا اللَّهُ وَحْدَهُ لَا شَرِيكَ لَهُ وَاللَّهُ أَكْبَرُ كَبِيرًا وَالْحَمْدُ لِلَّهِ كَثِيرًا وَسُبْحَانَ اللَّهِ / رَبِّ الْعَالَمِينَ وَلا حَوْلَ وَلا قُوَّةَ إِلا بِاللَّهِ الْعَزِيزِ الْحَكِيمِقَالَ هَؤُلاءِ لِرَبِّي فَمَا لِيقَالَ قُلِ اللَّهُمَّ اغْفِرْ لِي وَارْحَمْنِي وَاهْدِنِي وَارْزُقْنِي وَعَافِنِيهَذَا إِسْنَادٌ صَحِيحٌ أَخْرَجَهُ مُسْلِمٌ وَبِهِ يَقْوَى الْحَدِيثُ الَّذِي قبله وَالله سُبْحَانَهُ أعلم

**English**:
> Certainly, already it was narrated (ruwiya) from modern Saʿd ibn Abī Waqqāṣ (may Allah be pleased with him) also (ayḍan) informed us it Muḥammad ibn Aḥmad the Zarād and others I Yūsuf Ibn bint al-Jawzī said informed us [anā] Jadī the Imām Abū al-Faraj ʿAbd al-Raḥmān ibn al-Jawzī I ʿAlī ibn ʿAbd al-Wāḥid I al-Ḥasan ibn ʿAlī I ʿAlī ibn Kīsān the Grammarian (al-Naḥwī) narrated to us [thanā] Yūsuf ibn Yaʿqūb the Judge (al-Qāḍī) narrated to us [thanā] Muḥammad ibn Abī Bakr narrated to us [thanā] ʿUmar ibn ʿAlī narrated to us [thanā] Mūsā al-Jahnī from Muṣʿib ibn Saʿd Ibn Abī Waqqāṣ from his father (may Allah be pleased with him) said: came al-Aʿrābī to the Prophet (may Allah bless him and grant him peace) so he said knew me speech (kalām) say it said say not deity (ilāh) except Allah and defined it (wa-ḥaddahu) / alone (waḥdahu) not Sharīk for him By Allah greater (akbar) great, big praise to Allah abundantly Glory be to Allah of the worlds, all creatures and not power (ḥawl) and not strength (quwwah) except in Allah the Mighty (al-ʿAzīz) the All-Wise said these for O my Lord (Rabbī) so what for me me (lī) said say O Allah forgive for me me (lī) have mercy me and me Ahd provide me me Waʿāf this chain of transmission sound extracted it in his Hadith compilation (akhrajahu) Muslim and by it (wa-bihi) strength Modern the one who before him By Allah Glorified is He knows best
